  • Peter Kalemkerian, Baird: Could European CAP budget policy changes in 2028 alter 2027 demand, and what is the directional forecast for Europe in 2027? Chris Seibert stated it is too early to determine if proposed policy shifts will alter buying behavior, as visibility currently extends only four to five months.

Notable quotes

  • “As we discussed, the agricultural environment remains challenging, but we continue to believe that 2026 represents the bottom of the ag equipment cycle.” Brent Norwood, Chief Financial Officer
  • “At this time, results are up mid-single digits compared to the completion of last year's program.” Deanna Kovar, President, Worldwide Agriculture & Turf Division
  • “Customers are using See & Spray on significantly more acres year-over-year while achieving more than 50% herbicide savings.” Deanna Kovar, President, Worldwide Agriculture & Turf Division

What changed

Risks mentioned

  • European arable farmers face margin headwinds from weather stress and persistent high input costs.
  • Net tariff costs are expected to increase from approximately $750 million in FY 2026 to $1 billion in FY 2027, creating a year-over-year profit headwind.
  • Dynamic cumulative inflation requires disciplined pricing adjustments across multiple order windows.

Gemini 3.8 Flash6.0

Deere & Company delivered Q3 2026 net income of $1.379 billion and diluted EPS of $5.10, supported by double-digit revenue growth in Construction & Forestry and Small Ag & Turf offsetting large ag weakness. Management reaffirmed that fiscal 2026 represents the bottom of the agricultural equipment cycle, setting up expectations for a measured recovery in fiscal 2027. Full-year net income guidance was narrowed upward to $4.75 billion to $5.0 billion, even as direct tariffs remain a significant cost headwind.

Key takeaways

  • Deere reaffirmed that fiscal 2026 marks the cyclical bottom for agricultural equipment, though Deanna Kovar, Division President, expects a measured recovery rather than a sharp rebound in 2027.
  • Gross direct tariff expense guidance was lowered to $1.1 billion from $1.2 billion following Section 232 European rate cuts, but Brent Norwood, CFO, cautioned that net tariffs will step up to roughly $1 billion in fiscal 2027 due to the expiration of one-off refunds.
  • Construction & Forestry remains strong with order books extended four to five months out, driven by independent rental demand and dealer fleet expansions representing 30% to 35% of earthmoving transactions.
  • Early Order Program (EOP) intake for model year 2027 is tracking up mid-single digits, supported by strong precision adoption with See & Spray orders doubling year-over-year.
  • Production & Precision Ag and Construction & Forestry will modestly underproduce retail demand entering fiscal 2027 to maintain disciplined dealer inventory levels.

Key metrics

  • Net sales and revenues $12.608 billion up 5% YoY
  • Equipment operations net sales $10.999 billion up 6% YoY
  • Equipment operations operating margin 14.4% strong factory execution
  • Net income attributable to Deere & Company $1.379 billion Q3 reported
  • Diluted earnings per share $5.10 Q3 reported
  • Worldwide Financial Services net income $219 million higher YoY
  • Total engaged acres more than 520 million acres across nearly 1.2 million connected machines
  • Highly engaged acres more than 190 million acres double-digit growth YoY

Financial highlights

  • Total net sales and revenues rose 5% year-over-year to $12.608 billion, with equipment operations net sales up 6% to $10.999 billion.
  • Equipment operations generated an operating margin of 14.4% in Q3.
  • Production & Precision Ag net sales declined 6% year-over-year to $3.998 billion with an operating margin of 13.2% and positive price realization of 2.5 points.
  • Small Ag & Turf net sales rose 12% year-over-year to $3.383 billion, generating an operating margin of 18.4%.
  • Construction & Forestry net sales jumped 18% year-over-year to $3.618 billion, delivering an operating margin of 12.1% and 8 points of positive price realization.

Guidance

  • Dan Pooley, Manager of Investor Communications, guided FY 2026 Deere & Company net income to between $4.75 billion and $5.0 billion, narrowing from the prior range of $4.5 billion to $5.0 billion.
  • Equipment operations operating cash flows are expected to be between $5.0 billion and $5.5 billion for FY 2026, revised from $4.5 billion to $5.5 billion.
  • Chris Seibert, Director of Investor Relations, guided FY 2026 direct tariff expense to approximately $1.1 billion, reduced from $1.2 billion previously, alongside $382 million in total recognized IEPA refunds.
  • Brent Norwood, CFO, projected FY 2027 net tariff expense (direct tariffs minus refunds) to step up to around $1 billion, compared to approximately $750 million in FY 2026.
  • Dan Pooley expects Production & Precision Ag FY 2026 net sales to be down approximately 10% with operating margins between 11% and 12%, while Construction & Forestry net sales are guided up approximately 20% with operating margins between 10.5% and 11.5%.

Q&A

    answered directly
  • Kristen Owen, Oppenheimer: Did shipments slip between 3Q and 4Q, and why did tractor inventory build ahead of the industry in 3Q?
    Chris Seibert, Director of Investor Relations: Chris Seibert, Director of Investor Relations, pushed back on inventory concerns, stating demand was intentionally pulled into 3Q to de-risk 4Q, while Brent Norwood, CFO, added that 4Q sales for PPA and C&F will mirror 3Q but experience lower margins due to the absence of refunds and higher seasonal R&D and SA&G spending.

    answered directly

Rung: curated

Inputs are the latest transcript for each ticker (and the one before it) from the defeatbeta dataset, and live Yahoo profiles, pulled 2026-10-11. Each model was called once per input, so a model's score on a single ticker has noise; the averages across six calls and twelve tickers are the signal. Prices are list prices checked on October 11, 2026; Gemini Flash prices double on January 1, 2027.

Reproduce: node scripts/bakeoff/llm-bakeoff.mjs inputs|gen|gen2|check|judge (gen2 haiku-s,sonnet-s,opus-s,fable-s writes prompt files for subagents under scripts/bakeoff/sub/), node scripts/bakeoff/ladder.mjs, then node scripts/bakeoff/bakeoff-report.mjs.
